New Delhi-1 , the 5th August 1968 
No . 4 -CA ( 1 ) / 10 / 68-69. — In pursuance of Regulation 
16 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1964 , it is 
heiaby notified that in exercise of the powers conferred by 
clause ( a ) of sub - Section ( 1 ) of Section 20 of the 
Chartered Accountants Act , 1949, the Council of the 
Institute of Chartered Accountants of India , has removed 
from the Register of Members of this Institute , on account 
of death , with effect from the 22nd day of July , 1968, the 
name of Shri Sivar Krishnamoorthy of Seth Narayanadoss 
Lay out, Street No. 2 Coimbatore 12 , (Membership No. 
322 ) . 

No. 5 -CA ( 1 ) / 6 / 68 -69. - - With reference to this Insti 
tute s Notification No, 4 .CA ( 1 ) / 11/ 67 -68 , dated the 11th 
July, 1967 , it is hereby notified in pursuance of Regula 
tion 18 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations , 1964, 
that in exercise of the powers conferred by Rgulation 17 
of the said Regulations, the Council of the Institute of 
Chartered Accountants of India has restored to the Regis 
ter of Members, with cffect from the 13th September , 
1967, the name of Shri Adic Rustomjee Motafram , 
A . C . A ., of 63 , Belle View , 85 , Warden Road , Bombay - 26 , 
(Membership No. 2131 ) .

THE INSTITUTE OF COST AND WORKS 

ACCOUNTANTS OF INDIA 

( COST ACCOUNTANTS ) 

Culcutta, the 2nd August 1968 
No . 39 -CW A ( 28 ) /68 — In exercise of the powers con 
ferred by sub -section ( 1 ) of Section 39 of the Cost and 
Works Accountants Act, 1959, ( Act No . 23 of 1959 ) , 
the Council of the Institute of Cost and Works Accoun 
tants of India has made the following amendments in 
the Cost and Works Accountants Regulations, 1959, the 
same having been previously published and approved by 
the Central Government as required by sub -section ( 3 ) 
of the said Section : 

In the said Regulations : 

I. In sub -regulation (6 ) of Regulation 7 , for the exist 
ing last sentence , the following shall be substituted , name 
ly : 

" Tho amount so collected shall be transferred to 
the respective Regional Councils by the Council ." 
II. After the existing Regulation 23 and before the 
existing Regulation 24 , the following new Regulation 23A 
shall be inserted , namely : 

" 23A : Restoration to Studentship . -. - The Council 
may on request made to it by a person whose regis 
tration has been cancelled under Regulation 23, res 
tore his registration , if he is otherwise eligiblo 
thereto , on payment before such restoration the 
annual subscription for the year during which his 
registration is restored , all arrears on account of the 
annual subscription and a restoration fec of Rs. 15 : 

Provided that no restoration of registration shall 
he effected unless the request for such restoration is 
received within three years from the date of notify 
ing the cancellation of registration ." 
III . In sub -regulation ( 4 ) of Regulation 32 , the follow 
ing sub - clauses ( iii) and ( iv ) shall be added namely : 

" ( iii ) Those who have passed the Diploma/ Degree 
Examination of the Indian Institute of Science , Ban 
galore , with at least two papers or a paper of at 
least 200 maximum marks in Mathematics or Physics 
ay a subject shall be exempted from taking the paper 
on Practical Mathematics. 

( iv ) Thosc who have passed the Diploma/ Degree 
Examination in Engineering from the Indian Insti 
tute of Science , Bangalore , or from any of the 
Indian Institutes of Technology , shall be exempted 
from taking the papers on Practical Mathematics 

and Elements of Engineering ." 
IV . In sub -clause ( ii) of sub -regulation ( 4 ) of Regula 
tion 35 , for the words " or the Statistician s Diploma 
Examination of the Indian Statistical Institute, Calcutta ," 
the following words shall be substituted , namely : 

" or the Diploma/ Degree Examination in Statistics 
from the Indian Statistical Institute , Calcutta " . 
V . For the existing sub- regulation ( 5 ) of Regulation 
41, the following sub -regulation shall be suhstituted , 
namely : 

" ( 5 ) If after a period of six months from the 
date of declaration of results of an exámination a 
candidate requires a duplicate copy of his marksheet 
in respect of that examination , he shall be supplied 
with such duplicate copy of marksheet on receipt of 
an application from him in that behalf and on pay 
ment of Rs. 10 / - on account of fee for duplicate 
marksheet ; provided that the application for supply 
of duplicate copy of marksheet in respect of an 
examination shall be made within five years from 
the date of declaration of results of that examina 
tion . " 

S . N . GHOSE 

Secretary 


AGRICULTURAL REFINANCE CORPORATION 

NOTICE 
NOTICE is hereby given that the Fifth Annual Gencral 
Meeting of the Agricultural Refinance Corporation will be 
held at the Officers Lounge , Reserve Bank of India 
Building ( 3rd floor ) Bombay on Friday, September 27 , 
1968 at 3 P . M . to transact the following business ; - - 
( a ) to discuss the auditej annual accounts for tho 

year ended June 30 , 1968; 
( b ) to disluss the auditor s report on the annual 

balance sheet and accounts ; 
( c ) to discuss the report of the Board on the work 

ing of the Corporation for the year ended June 

30 , 1968; 
( d ) to elect one Director under clause (d ) of Section 

10 of the Agricultural Refinance Corporation 
Act by such of the central land mortgage banks 
us are sharcholders of the Corporation in place 
of Shri Udayabhansinghji who retires in terms of 
Section 11 ( 2 ) of the Act, 
to clect one Director under clause ( e ) of Section 
10 of the Agricultural Refinance Corporation 
Act by such of the State cooperative banks as 
are shareholders of the Corporation in place of 
Shri M . R . Patel, who retires in terms of Section 

11 ( 3 ) of the Act. 
( 0) to elect one Director under clause (f ) of Section 

10 of the Agricultural Refinance Corporation , 
Act, by tho Life Insurance Corporation , schedul 
od banks, insurance and investment companies 
and other financial institutions, which are share 
holders of the Corporation in place of Shri M . 
G . Parikh who retires in terms of Section 11 ( 3 ) 

of the Act. 
The share register of the Corporation will remain 
closed from Thursday , September 12 , 1968 to Thursday , 
September 26 , 1968 both days inclusive ,
